- **2026-06-13 续作：M8 object placement contract（无数据迁移）**：新增 `objects.placement_set_id/placement_pool_id/placement_topology_hash` 迁移（中文 COMMENT + placement_set 索引注释），并引入 `Maxio.Cluster.Placement` 作为对象真实落点解析门面。`Maxio.Storage.Distributed` 写入/assemble 会返回实际 set placement；S3 PutObject/CopyObject/CompleteMultipartUpload、Console upload/create-folder、delete marker 与 lifecycle marker 会把 placement 落入对象元数据。GET/download/delete/version delete/lifecycle hard delete 会把对象行中的 `placement_set_id` 作为 storage opts 传回后端，避免 topology 扩容后按新 hash 读/删错 set。`Maxio.Cluster.Rebalance` 现优先使用对象持久化 placement，并对旧行或未知落点输出 `placement_fallbacks`；`mix maxio.rebalance.plan` text/json 同步暴露该字段。语义说明：这是 rebalance/decommission 的落点契约与只读估算纠偏，不移动 shard、不实现 job/status/stop、不改变 free-space placement；没有 placement 的历史行仍按当前 router fallback 并显式计数。独立 reviewer 首轮指出 Console object detail 删除具体版本未删存储层且未传 placement；已修复为 PG 删除成功后 best-effort `Storage.delete/4` 并补捕获后端回归，同时把 Distributed 未知 placement 从 MatchError 收敛为结构化 `{:error, {:unknown_set_id, ...}}`；复审 `APPROVED_WITH_NOTES`，仅余后续可选硬化：Console best-effort storage delete 失败可补结构化 warn。验证：`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；review-fix focused `mix test test/maxio_web/live/object_live_show_test.exs test/maxio/storage/distributed_placement_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/placement_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_plan_test.exs` → 22 passed；相邻调用面 `mix test test/maxio/storage/distributed_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/cluster_e2e_test.exs test/maxio/s3/multipart_integration_test.exs test/maxio/multipart_test.exs test/maxio/s3/slow_down_quorum_test.exs test/maxio_web/live/object_live_index_test.exs test/maxio_web/controllers/download_controller_test.exs test/maxio/lifecycle_test.exs` → 54 passed；`git diff --check` / `mix format --check-formatted` 通过；`mix precommit` → 1206 tests, 0 failures, 4 excluded。
- **2026-06-13 续作：M8 persistent rebalance/decommission job control plane（无数据迁移）**：新增 `cluster_rebalance_jobs` 迁移（表/列/索引/timestamps 均中文 COMMENT，含 active job partial unique index）、`Maxio.Cluster.Rebalance.Job` schema 与 `Maxio.Cluster.Rebalance.Jobs` context。`Jobs.start_rebalance/1` / `start_decommission/2` 会持久化 planner 快照、job kind/target set、cursor/progress 初始字段与 `data_movement=false` / `data_movement_status=not_started`；`cancel/2` / `cancel_latest/1` 只转换 planned/running/cancel_requested job，不搬迁 shard。新增 `mix maxio.rebalance.start [--decommission-set N] [--current-only] [--page-size N] [--no-drive-stats] [--json]`、`mix maxio.rebalance.status [--job-id UUID] [--all] [--decommission-set N] [--json]`、`mix maxio.rebalance.cancel [JOB_ID|--job-id UUID] [--decommission-set N] [--json]`。语义说明：这是 MinIO-style rebalance/decommission 的持久化运维控制面和取证边界；同一时间最多一个 active job；若计划存在 `placement_fallbacks` 会 fail-closed 拒绝创建 job，避免在真实落点不明时生成可执行假计划；decommission job 拒绝 `current_only`，必须盘点全版本/delete marker。当前不启动 worker、不移动 shard、不删除源 shard、不改变对象路由、pool 选择或 free-space placement。验证：`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_jobs_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_job_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_plan_test.exs` → 24 passed；`git diff --check` / `mix format --check-formatted` 通过；`mix precommit` → 1221 tests, 0 failures, 4 excluded；独立 reviewer 首轮发现 decommission + `current_only` 会持久化不完整 job，已修复为建计划前 fail-closed 并补 context/CLI 回归，复审 `APPROVED_WITH_NOTES`，仅余后续可选硬化：DB partial unique race path 可在引入 runner/worker 前补并发竞争测试。

- **2026-06-13 续作：M8 rebalance runner singleton lock + monotonic progress/cursor**：`Maxio.Cluster.Rebalance.Runner.run_once/2` 非 dry-run 现在先获取 job 级 quorum lock（resource `{:rebalance_job_runner, job_id}`），锁内重新 `Jobs.get/1` 并重新校验 job kind/status/destination/topology/PG authority；锁不可得时 fail-closed 返回 `:runner_locked` 并打结构化 error 日志，CLI 输出明确错误。`Jobs.record_progress/3` 改为 SQL 级 monotonic update：progress counters 使用 `GREATEST`，cursor 只有在 `progress_scanned` 严格前进时才更新，stale foreground runner 不能回退 progress/cursor。语义说明：这是 source cleanup / 后台 worker 前的执行边界硬化；不删除源 shard、不启动后台 worker、不改 topology/pool/free-space placement，仍不是完整 MinIO decommission。验证：`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_jobs_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_runner_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_run_test.exs test/maxio/objects/placement_update_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_job_test.exs test/maxio/storage/distributed_placement_test.exs` → 29 tests, 0 failures；`git diff --check` 通过；`mix precommit` → 1233 tests, 0 failures, 4 excluded；独立 review `APPROVED_WITH_NOTES`（无阻塞，补 dry-run 持锁仍只读回归）。
- **2026-06-13 续作：M8 guarded decommission source-shard cleanup**：新增 `cluster_rebalance_jobs.source_cleanup_*` 独立 cursor/counter 迁移（中文 COMMENT），`source_cleanup_status` 扩展 `moving`；新增 `Maxio.Cluster.Rebalance.SourceCleanup` 与 `mix maxio.rebalance.cleanup [JOB_ID|--job-id <uuid>] [--max-versions N] [--page-size N] [--dry-run] [--json]`。cleanup 起初只支持 `data_movement_status=complete` 的 decommission job（后续已扩展 rebalance，见 guarded rebalance source-shard cleanup 条目）；非 dry-run 复用 job-level quorum lock（`{:rebalance_job_runner, job_id}`），锁内重新读取 job 并进入 `source_cleanup_status=moving`；每个版本在对象锁内重新读取 PG row，只有当前 `placement_set_id == destination_set_id` 时才通过 `Storage.delete_version/4` 指定 `placement_set_id: target_set_id` 删除旧 source set 副本。PG placement 仍是 target 时 fail-closed 并标记 cleanup failed；placement 缺失或非 destination 时跳过；删除 quorum 失败会进入 `source_cleanup_status=failed`，`--max-versions` 截断保持 moving 并用独立 cleanup cursor resume，dry-run 不删 shard、不改 job；cleanup 完成前会做一次 PG target-placement final guard，若仍有 row 指向 target set 则 fail-closed，不标 complete。取消 cleanup 时保留 `data_movement_status=complete` 并把 `source_cleanup_status` 置为 `skipped`，避免破坏已完成的 copy-pass 状态。语义说明：这是已迁移版本的旧副本清理，不删 PG object row，不冻结新写，不证明 target set 已空，不改 topology/pool/free-space placement，不是完整 MinIO pool removal/decommission。验证：`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_source_cleanup_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_jobs_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_cleanup_test.exs` → 21 tests, 0 failures；相邻 rebalance 批次 `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_runner_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_run_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_job_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_plan_test.exs test/maxio/objects/placement_update_test.exs` → 19 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` / `git diff --check` 通过；`mix precommit` → 1243 tests, 0 failures, 4 excluded；独立 review 首轮发现 cleanup cancel 会破坏 data movement 状态、completion 缺少 final target-placement guard，均已修复并补回归；复审 `APPROVED_WITH_NOTES`，仅余已文档化残余风险：未冻结新写，final guard 不是事务级 target-set-empty 证明。
- **2026-06-13 续作：M8 decommission write-routing freeze / MPU placement contract**：新增 `Maxio.Cluster.Rebalance.WriteRouting` 与 `Placement.resolve_write/3`，从 active decommission job 的 target/destination 状态推导写入排除表；普通新写、delete marker、CompleteMultipartUpload 最终对象写入会避开正在退役的 target set，显式 `placement_set_id` 仍绝对优先，GET/HEAD/delete/version delete/source cleanup 仍使用持久化 placement 或普通 router，不被 write-routing 改写。新增 `multipart_uploads.placement_set_id/placement_pool_id/placement_topology_hash` 迁移（中文 COMMENT + 索引注释），Initiate MPU 冻结 upload temp 落点，UploadPart/Complete 读 part/Abort 使用 upload placement，最终对象 placement 仍按目标 object key 当前写路由解析，避免 part 与最终对象被混淆。CopyObject 自拷贝临时对象、S3/PG delete marker 与 lifecycle delete marker 统一传递同一次 write placement，lifecycle AbortIncompleteMultipartUpload 也会按 upload placement 清理 part。语义说明：这是 decommission data movement/cleanup 后的写入冻结边界，不变更 topology/pool/free-space placement，不证明 target set 为空，不替代后台 decommission worker；write-routing DB 不可用时生产 fail-closed，测试环境仅对非 DB owner 场景 fail-open 并打 debug 日志。独立 review 首轮发现 lifecycle marker/MPU abort 丢 opts、CreateMultipartUpload 对 write-routing 错误会 MatchError，均已修复并补回归；复审无阻塞。验证：post-review focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_write_routing_test.exs test/maxio/lifecycle_test.exs test/maxio/s3/multipart_integration_test.exs test/maxio/multipart_test.exs` → 35 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` / `mix format --check-formatted` / `git diff --check` 通过；`mix precommit` → 1251 tests, 0 failures, 4 excluded。
- **2026-06-13 续作：M8 config-gated decommission/rebalance scheduler（后台推进器）**：新增 `Maxio.Cluster.Rebalance.Scheduler` 并接入监督树，默认 disabled。启用 `config :maxio, :rebalance_scheduler, enabled: true` 后，scheduler 在 cluster singleton lock（`{:rebalance_scheduler, :run}`）下选择下一个可推进 rebalance/decommission job：先推进 planned/running copy-pass（调用既有 `Runner.run_once/2`），copy 完成后在无其它 active job 阻挡时推进 guarded source cleanup（调用既有 `SourceCleanup.cleanup_once/2`）。每个实际数据阶段仍复用 job-level lock（`{:rebalance_job_runner, job_id}`）与原状态机；scheduler 只做编排，不新增第二套迁移/清理逻辑。`run_now/1` 与 `status/0` 暴露给 IEx/测试；`destination_set_id`/`destination_sets`/`auto_destination` 只作用于 decommission，rebalance job 会剥离这些 destination opts 并继续逐对象按 current router/topology 选目标。非 idle 错误会结构化 `Log.warn(action: :rebalance_scheduler, reason: ...)` 留痕，`no_runnable_job` 保持低噪声；错误结果会清空旧 `last_result`。语义说明：这是 MinIO-style decommission/rebalance 后台 drain 的一个受控入口；仍不做 topology/pool mutation、pool removal 或完成后部署配置改写。官方 MinIO/AIStor 文档强调 decommission 会将目标 pool 置于 draining/read-only、读正常、写路由到剩余 pool，并基于剩余 pool free space 自动迁移；本轮只覆盖“后台推进已有 copy/cleanup primitive”这一层，最终移除仍 defer。验证：focused `mix test - **2026-06-13 续作：M8 free-space decommission destination selection（受限目的 set 自动选择）**：新增 `Maxio.Cluster.Rebalance.Destination.select_decommission_destination/2`，基于 `DriveStats.sample_topology/1` 的 filesystem free-space telemetry 为 decommission copy-pass 选择一个非 target set。selector fail-closed：无 topology、未知 target、无非 target set、candidate endpoint 样本缺失、candidate endpoint error、健康候选空间不足都会返回结构化错误；候选必须样本数等于 topology/configured endpoint 数，且所有 endpoint ok，`drive_free_bytes` 与 `drive_min_free_bytes` 均满足 `plan_bytes` 要求，最终按 total free、min free、低 set id 稳定选择。`Runner.run_once/2` 新增 `auto_destination: true`，优先级保持 explicit destination > persisted destination > auto destination；report/日志/CLI 暴露 `free_space_placement`。`Scheduler` 默认 `auto_destination: true`，但显式 `destination_set_id`/`destination_sets` 仍优先，cleanup 阶段不重选目的地；`mix maxio.rebalance.run --auto-destination` 支持前台/干跑取证。语义说明：这是 bounded decommission destination picker，只为既有 copy-pass 选单个目的 set；不做多目的地分布、全局 rebalance free-space policy、topology/pool mutation、pool removal，也不删除 source shard。验证：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_destination_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_runner_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_scheduler_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_run_test.exs` → 28 tests, 0 failures；相邻 rebalance 批次 `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_destination_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_runner_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_scheduler_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_run_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_jobs_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_source_cleanup_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_write_routing_test.exs` → 54 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` / `mix format --check-formatted` / `git diff --check` 通过；`mix precommit` → 1271 tests, 0 failures, 4 excluded；独立 review 首轮指出 selector 应校验 telemetry 样本数等于 topology endpoint 数，已补 `drive_expected/drive_missing` 与缺失样本回归，复审 `APPROVED`。
- **2026-06-13 续作：M8 pool metadata placement contract（pool-level rebalance 前置）**：`Maxio.Cluster.Topology.parse_and_validate!/1` 现在保留 `pool_ids`、`pools[pool_id].set_ids` 与 `sets[set_id].pool_id`，全局 `set_ids` 改为严格按部署 pool 顺序 + set 顺序展开；新增 `pool_ids(_from)`、`set_ids_for_pool(_from)`、`pool_id_for_set(_from)` helper。旧 RouteB/FS legacy topology map 仅在完全没有 `pool_ids`/`pools` 且 set entry 没有 `pool_id` 时回退为单 pool 0；半新半旧 pool metadata 会 fail-fast，避免 hash 与 placement pool_id 分叉。`Placement.resolve/3`、`resolve_write/3`、显式 `placement_set_id` override 与 decommission redirect 都会在 topology 可用时填充正确 `pool_id`，`topology_hash` 纳入 pool membership，`FS.local_topology/1` 也升级为完整 pool shape。语义说明：这是 pool-level free-space rebalance、pool removal 与多 pool 二级路由的元数据地基；本轮不改变实际 hash 路由算法，不做 free-space policy，不迁移对象，不改 topology。验证：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/topology_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/router_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/placement_test.exs test/maxio/storage/distributed_placement_test.exs` → 23 tests, 0 failures；相邻批次 `mix test test/maxio/object_meta_shadow_test.exs test/maxio/object_meta_read_authority_test.exs test/maxio/object_meta_list_authority_test.exs test/maxio/object_meta_list_shadow_test.exs test/maxio/listing_test.exs test/maxio/storage/scanner_test.exs test/maxio/routeb_b6_2_fs_authority_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_jobs_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_runner_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_source_cleanup_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_write_routing_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_destination_test.exs test/maxio/storage/distributed_test.exs test/maxio/storage/distributed_conformance_test.exs` → 125 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` / `mix format --check-formatted` / `git diff --check` 通过；`mix precommit` → 1277 tests, 0 failures, 4 excluded；独立 review 首轮指出 legacy pool fallback 过宽，已收紧为完整新 shape 或真 legacy shape，复审 `APPROVED`。
- **2026-06-13 续作：M8 pool free-space weighted new-write placement（MinIO server-pool 新写语义）**：新增 `Maxio.Cluster.Placement.PoolWritePolicy`，`Placement.resolve_write/3` 在普通新写（无显式 `placement_set_id`）时可按 pool filesystem free bytes 加权选择 pool，再在该 pool 的 set 列表内按对象名 hash 选择 set；显式 placement override 仍绝对优先，读/删/旧对象仍优先使用持久化 placement。默认 `config :maxio, :pool_write_policy, mode: :hash` 保持既有全局 set hash；运维可通过 `mode: :weighted_free_space` 或 `MAXIO_POOL_WRITE_POLICY=weighted_free_space` 显式开启，`MAXIO_POOL_WRITE_MAX_USED_PERCENT_X100` 控制 99% 默认高水位保护。策略 fail-closed：candidate pool endpoint telemetry 缺失或 endpoint error 会 fail-closed；过高使用率或无可用空间的 pool 会被跳过，直到无可写 pool 时返回结构化错误；可传 `drive_stats` 快照避免每次写入现场采样，当前未内建后台 telemetry cache，未传快照时仍会在写路径现场采样。随后仍应用既有 decommission `WriteRouting`，因此退役 set redirect 只作为兜底，active decommission source set 已从加权候选与 telemetry completeness 中 set-aware 排除。语义说明：这是 MinIO-style server-pool 扩容后的“新对象按 pool 可用空间比例落点”基础，不迁移旧对象、不改 topology、不做 pool removal，也不替代完整 rebalance/decommission workflow。验证：post-review focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/pool_write_policy_test.exs test/maxio/s3/pool_write_policy_error_test.exs` → 8 tests, 0 failures；相邻 placement/rebalance 批次 `mix test test/maxio/cluster/placement_test.exs test/maxio/storage/distributed_placement_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_destination_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_write_routing_test.exs` → 22 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` / `mix format --check-formatted` / `git diff --check` 通过；post-review `mix precommit` → 1285 tests, 0 failures, 4 excluded；独立 `Lorentz` review 首轮指出 incomplete telemetry 未全局 fail-closed、S3 错误可能泄露 placement internals、无效 `MAXIO_POOL_WRITE_POLICY` 静默降级，均已修复并补回归；独立 `Archimedes` post-fix review 指出 decommission source sets 仍参与 weighted selection 与 docs 高水位 wording，均已修复并补回归；独立 `Boole` final re-review 指出 write-routing structural tuple 仍可泄露到 Object XML，已补 generic InternalError 映射和端到端泄露回归。
- **2026-06-13 续作：M8 bounded foreground rebalance copy-pass（旧对象按当前 topology/router 迁移）**：`Maxio.Cluster.Rebalance.Runner` 现支持 `kind="rebalance"` job。rebalance job 不绑定单一 `destination_set_id`，`Jobs.begin_rebalance_data_movement/2` 只把 job 置为 `running/moving` 并把 `source_cleanup_status` 标为 `not_started`；runner 在 PG read/list 权威下枚举全版本/delete marker，对每行用 `Placement.resolve/3`（不是 `resolve_write/3`）按当前 topology/router 计算目标 set，仅当持久化 placement 与目标 set 不一致时，在对象锁内重新读取、从原 `Placement.storage_opts(object)` 读源、写入目标 `placement_set_id`，目标写成功后用 `Objects.update_placement_if_current/2` 乐观切换 PG placement。已在目标 set 的行计 `skipped` 并推进 cursor，确保 `--max-versions` 可穿过大量已正确行；缺失持久化 placement 会 fail-closed，不按 router 猜源；`--destination-set`/`--auto-destination` 对 rebalance job 明确报错。delete marker copy 保留 sidecar modtime/generation；对象 copy 保留 version id、metadata/tags/etag/size/SSE 摘要；源 shard 由单独 guarded source cleanup 清理。语义说明：这是旧对象的受限 rebalance copy-pass；runner 本身是前台 primitive，后台 scheduler 已可调用同一 primitive（见 scheduler 条目），但仍不改 topology/pool、不做 pool removal、不使用 pool free-space/write-routing 策略，也不证明完整 MinIO rebalance/decommission workflow。验证：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_runner_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_run_test.exs` → 17 tests, 0 failures；相邻 rebalance/placement 批次 `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_jobs_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_runner_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_scheduler_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_source_cleanup_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_write_routing_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_destination_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/placement_test.exs test/maxio/storage/distributed_placement_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/pool_write_policy_test.exs test/maxio/s3/pool_write_policy_error_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_run_test.exs` → 78 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；独立 `Kuhn` planning lane 确认正确边界/不变量（使用 `Placement.resolve/3`、缺 placement fail-closed、cursor 需推进 skipped 行）。

- **2026-06-13 续作：M8 guarded rebalance source-shard cleanup**：`Maxio.Cluster.Rebalance.SourceCleanup` 与 `mix maxio.rebalance.cleanup` 现同时支持 `kind="rebalance"` job。rebalance cleanup 只允许 `data_movement_status=complete` 的 job，复用 job-level quorum lock 与对象锁；每个版本重新读取 PG row，PG `placement_set_id` 是唯一权威保留 set，cleanup 会对当前 topology 中其它 set 的同 `bucket/key/version_id` 调用 `Storage.delete_version/4` 做 quorum 删除。缺失 PG placement 或 PG placement 指向未知 set 会 fail-closed，不按 router 猜；dry-run 不删 shard、不改 job；独立 cleanup cursor/counter 支持 `--max-versions` resume。完成前 final guard 会拒绝仍有缺失/未知 placement 的 PG row。scheduler 第二轮可自动推进 rebalance source cleanup。语义说明：这是已迁移版本的旧副本清理，不是 orphan shard scanner，不证明 pool/set 物理为空，不改 topology/pool，不做 pool removal 或部署配置改写。验证：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_source_cleanup_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_runner_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_scheduler_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_cleanup_test.exs` → 36 tests, 0 failures；相邻 rebalance/placement/CLI 批次 `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_jobs_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_runner_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_scheduler_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_source_cleanup_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_write_routing_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_destination_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/placement_test.exs test/maxio/storage/distributed_placement_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/pool_write_policy_test.exs test/maxio/s3/pool_write_policy_error_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_run_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_cleanup_test.exs` → 85 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` / `mix format --check-formatted` / `git diff --check` 通过；`mix precommit` → 1298 tests, 0 failures, 4 excluded。

[--expected-current-topology-hash <sha256>] [--json]`。`apply_removal` 在 global runtime-topology quorum lock 内重新执行 `removal_plan/2`（因此继承 preflight 的 active job、target placement、missing/unknown placement、active MPU、endpoint、physical residual blockers），可选校验 expected current topology hash，先记录 `topology_removal_status=runtime_local_applied`、scope、before/after hash 与 applied_at 审计字段；若 audit 已记录且 before/after hash 与当前 plan 一致，重复执行会继续完成 runtime install，用于恢复 audit 后、runtime install 前的 lease-loss/进程中断窗口，hash 不一致则仍 fail-closed。随后把 proposed topology 写入当前 BEAM runtime；`Jobs.start*` 也会获取同一 - **2026-06-14 续作：M8 scheduler cadence/overdue observer v1（后台调度活性只读观察器）**：`SchedulerRuns.cadence_report/1` 与 `mix maxio.scheduler.status --cadence` 只读聚合现有 run ledger/state，输出每个 scheduler 的 scheduled-only health、窗口内 scheduled/effective/skipped/blocked/failed 计数、最近 scheduled/effective/healthy run、overdue age 与 stale reason。manual `run_now/1` 不参与后台活性证明；`lock_unavailable` 与 backoff 等 skipped scheduled row 不能作为绿色 heartbeat；latest scheduled `failed/blocked` 不会被后续 manual recovery 清绿；alert/backoff/effective failed/blocked state 优先于最近有 run。未实现 retention/prune：独立 Godel design lane 指出缺 durable rollup 契约前删除 ledger 会削弱生产取证，因此本轮选择 observer 而非 retention。验证：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/scheduler_runs_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_status_test.exs` → 19 tests, 0 failures；scheduler 聚焦 `mix test test/maxio/cluster/scheduler_runs_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/scheduler_cadence_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/scheduler_peer_cadence_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/fresh_drive_heal_scheduler_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_scheduler_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_status_test.exs` → 65 tests, 0 failures, 1 excluded；相邻 replacement/rebalance workflow + fresh-drive CLI 批次 → 21 tests, 0 failures。独立 Gauss review 首轮指出 scheduled failed 后 manual ok 会误报 green 的 HIGH，已拆分 effective scheduled evidence 与 healthy scheduled heartbeat（只有 scheduled ok/idle 可变绿）并补回归。
- **2026-06-14 续作：M8 scheduler run ledger durable rollup contract（retention/prune 前置审计摘要）**：新增 `cluster_scheduler_run_rollups`（中文 COMMENT 自文档化 schema）与 `Maxio.Cluster.SchedulerRunRollup`，按 `scheduler + trigger + window_started_at + window_ms` 幂等 upsert 半开窗口 `[start, end)` 的 run 摘要。rollup 记录 run/status/result/effective/healthy/duration 计数，first/last/effective/healthy run 指针，裁剪且 redacted 的 `last_summary`，`source_max_updated_at` 与 `source_digest`；空窗口也可持久化 `run_count=0`，便于后续 prune 前证明“已审计到无事件”。新增写 CLI `mix maxio.scheduler.rollup --from <iso8601> --until <iso8601> [--scheduler fresh_drive_heal|rebalance] [--trigger manual|scheduled] [--window-minutes N] [--dry-run] [--json]`，以及 `mix maxio.scheduler.status --rollups [--trigger manual|scheduled] [--rollup-limit N]` 只读查看。语义边界：只汇总已关闭、UTC epoch-aligned 窗口，不删除 raw run ledger，不改 scheduler cadence/backoff/state，不启用 scheduler，不移动数据。验证按效率策略收窄：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/scheduler_runs_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_rollup_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_status_test.exs` → 28 tests, 0 failures；`mix format --check-formatted`（当前 rollup 切片文件）、`mix compile --warnings-as-errors`、`git diff --check` 通过；未跑全量 `mix precommit`。独立 Leibniz design lane 建议先补 durable rollup 再谈 retention/prune；独立 Plato review 首轮指出 microsecond alignment、invalid window_ms 默认化、invalid scheduler dry-run 三个 MEDIUM，均已修复并补回归。
- **2026-06-14 续作：M8 scheduler run ledger verified prune（rollup 复核后的明细清理）**：新增 `mix maxio.scheduler.prune` 与 `SchedulerRuns.prune_preflight/1` / `prune/1`。Prune 必须显式指定单个 scheduler + trigger + aligned closed window range；`--dry-run` 只做 preflight，真实删除必须 `--confirm-prune --confirm-scheduler <same> --confirm-trigger <same> --confirm-from <same> --confirm-until <same> --confirm-window-minutes <same>`。`record/6`、实际 `rollup_window/5` 与 prune 在各自 DB transaction 内共享 scheduler+trigger 级 PostgreSQL advisory lock；每个 prune 窗口还会锁定 rollup row 与候选 raw rows，重新计算 count/source_max_updated_at/source_digest 并与 durable rollup 完全一致后，只删除复核过的 id 集合；`record/6` 写 run 前也会锁定覆盖该 `finished_at` 的 rollup row，若窗口已 prune 则拒绝 late raw row。缺 rollup、digest/count/updated_at 不一致、open/current window、已 prune 后出现 late rows、命中 `cluster_scheduler_states.last_run_id` 或 latest scheduled/effective/healthy/latest scheduler+trigger raw run，都会 fail-closed；range 内任一窗口失败则整次 range all-or-nothing 回滚。Rollup 记录新增 `pruned_at/pruned_run_count/pruned_source_digest/pruned_by_node` 审计字段；已 prune 的窗口禁止被 `rollup` 重算覆盖。语义边界：不启用自动 retention policy，不改变 scheduler state/cadence/backoff/alert，不删除 rollup 摘要，不移动数据。验证按效率策略收窄：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/scheduler_runs_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_prune_test.exs` → 27 tests, 0 failures；相邻 CLI 批次 `mix test test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_rollup_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_status_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_prune_test.exs` → 12 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；独立 Boyle design lane 建议显式确认与 all-or-nothing prune；独立 Beauvoir review 首轮指出 record/prune 串行化、destructive range confirm、latest ordering 三处问题，均已修复。
- **2026-06-14 续作：M8 scheduler ledger retention policy（默认关闭的旧明细清理编排）**：新增 `config :maxio, :scheduler_ledger_retention, enabled: false, retain_days: nil, window_minutes: 60, max_windows: 8784`、`SchedulerRuns.retention_plan/1` / `retention_prune/1` 与 CLI `mix maxio.scheduler.retention --scheduler <fresh_drive_heal|rebalance> --trigger <manual|scheduled> --retain-days N [--window-minutes N] --dry-run [--now <iso8601>] [--json]`。Retention 只计算 cutoff 之前的 UTC epoch-aligned closed windows，并复用现有 verified prune 内核；dry-run 在缺 rollup 时返回 blocked plan / missing_rollups，真实删除必须显式 `--enable-retention --confirm-retention --confirm-scheduler <same> --confirm-trigger <same> --confirm-retain-days <same> --confirm-window-minutes <same> --confirm-cutoff <computed>`，且 CLI destructive path 禁止 `--now` 覆盖 wall clock，API 也要求 `enabled: true` 与 `confirm?: true`。语义边界：不自动创建 rollup，不删除 rollup rows，不改 scheduler state/cadence/backoff/alert，不启用后台 retention timer，不接 heal/rebalance/topology/physical 自动化；缺 rollup、rollup mismatch、protected run、open/current window 等仍由 prune fail-closed。验证按效率策略收窄：`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/scheduler_runs_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_retention_test.exs` → 31 tests, 0 failures；相邻 CLI `mix test test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_rollup_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_prune_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_retention_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_status_test.exs` → 15 tests, 0 failures；独立 Hume review 指出 destructive CLI `--now` 可把 cutoff 推到未来，已改为 `--now` 仅 dry-run 可用并补回归；暂未跑 `mix precommit`。
- **2026-06-14 续作：M8 scheduler 长跑证据补强（node 参与度 + blocked next_action 入 ledger）**：`SchedulerRuns.cadence_report/1` / `mix maxio.scheduler.status --cadence` 现在输出 `scheduled_nodes/effective_scheduled_nodes/healthy_scheduled_nodes/skipped_scheduled_nodes/lock_loser_nodes/lock_loser_count/last_effective_scheduled_node`，便于真实多节点长跑后判断哪些节点参与 scheduled pass、哪个节点产生有效 heartbeat、哪些只是 singleton lock loser。Cadence latest/window 查询统一限制 `finished_at <= now`，避免时钟偏斜或未来 run 伪造健康心跳。`@summary_keys` 增加 `next_action`，`Rebalance.Closure` report 透传 workflow `next_action`，使 auto closure blocked scheduled run 的修复命令进入 scheduler ledger summary；summary redaction 增强 `--token VALUE` / `--access-key VALUE` CLI 参数遮蔽，继续受 max summary bytes 约束。语义边界：只增强观测和 operator 修复提示，不自动修 PG placement/MPU，不触发 topology/physical/destructive automation，不改变 scheduler 主状态机。验证按效率策略收窄：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/scheduler_runs_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_status_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_scheduler_test.exs` → 68 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors`、目标文件 `mix format --check-formatted`、`git diff --check` 通过；独立 Maxwell design lane 建议此 slice；独立 Hegel review 首轮指出 future-dated run 会被当作心跳、`next_action` CLI secret 参数可能泄漏，均已修复并补回归，复审 no blocker/high/medium；暂未跑 `mix precommit`。

- **2026-06-14 续作：M8 blocked rebalance closure next_action 映射（operator 修复闭环提示）**：`Maxio.Cluster.Rebalance.Workflow.summary/2` 现在让 `rebalance_closure` 阶段的 `next_action` 依赖 live blockers 与 persisted closure failure：job/data/source/closure/topology/physical failure 优先指向 `mix maxio.rebalance.status --job-id <job-id> --json`；`unsupported_authoritative_source` / `runtime_topology_unavailable` 指向只读 `rebalance.workflow` 重查；`active_job_exists` 指向 active job 的 workflow；object placement 与 MPU placement/hash blocker 指向 `mix maxio.rebalance.verify_closure --job-id <job-id> --json`，供 operator 修复/完成/abort 后复核；只有纯 `routing_mismatch` 才指向 `mix maxio.rebalance.start --json` 创建新的普通 rebalance 计划；clean closure 仍为 `none`。`mix maxio.rebalance.workflow` 文本输出新增 `blockers=`，JSON 继续暴露机器可读 blockers/audit，`Maxio.Cluster.Replacement.Workflow` 默认 audit 透传该 next_action。语义边界：不新增 `operator_repair_hint` 第二事实源，不自动改 PG placement/hash，不自动 abort MPU，不重开 complete job cursor/status，不触发 decommission topology mutation、deployment finalization、physical remove/purge。验证：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_workflow_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_workflow_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/replacement_workflow_test.exs` → 19 tests, 0 failures；相邻 `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_workflow_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_scheduler_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_jobs_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_runner_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_source_cleanup_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/replacement_workflow_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_workflow_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_verify_closure_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_replacement_workflow_test.exs` → 97 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors`、`mix format --check-formatted`、`git diff --check` 通过。独立 Mill design lane 建议不新增单一 repair hint、不接自动修复；独立 Pauli review 首轮指出 persisted closure failed 会被 live audit 掩盖的 HIGH，已新增 `rebalance_closure_failed` failure blocker 与回归，复审 no blocker/high/medium。

- **2026-06-14 续作：M8 replacement live rehearsal scheduler-evidence bundle（手动闭环 + 后台证据同 artifact）**：`Maxio.Cluster.ReplacementLiveRehearsal` 新增默认关闭的 scheduler evidence bundle。默认仍只 shell out 到 `mix maxio.replacement.workflow --json --fail-on-blocked`，artifact 原有字段与 exit 语义保持；显式 `--require-scheduler-evidence` 后，再调用现有 `mix maxio.scheduler.evidence --json --fail-on-attention`，并转发前缀化参数 `--scheduler-evidence-scheduler/from/until/since-minutes/window-minutes/expected-nodes/expected-interval-ms/stale-after-ms/min-effective-nodes/require-lock-loser/require-rollups`。该实现不调用 `SchedulerRuns`，不复制 scheduler evidence 判定，只消费 CLI JSON/exit；blocked、attention、unknown status、warnings、非零 exit、不可解析 JSON 均 fail-closed。artifact 现在区分 `gate`（本次 replacement workflow gate）、`scheduler_evidence_gate`（后台 scheduled-only 证据 gate）与 `bundle_gate`（最终退出依据），并归档 `commands.scheduler_evidence`、`scheduler_evidence_exit_status`、`scheduler_evidence`、`scheduler_evidence_raw_output`；scheduler blocker 不写入 `replacement_workflow.blockers`，避免把“手动 replacement 是否完成”和“后台 scheduler 是否健康”混为一谈。README 与 `docs/superpowers/runbooks/m8_replacement_live_rehearsal.md` 已补同一 artifact 打包示例和防假绿边界。验证：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/replacement_live_rehearsal_test.exs` → 12 tests, 0 failures；相邻 `mix test test/maxio/cluster/replacement_live_rehearsal_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/scheduler_live_rehearsal_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_evidence_test.exs` → 22 tests, 0 failures；`mix run --no-start scripts/m8_replacement_live_rehearsal.exs -- --help` smoke 通过；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors`、scoped `git diff --check`、目标文件行尾空白检查均通过。独立 James design lane 建议做证据打包而非改变 workflow 语义；Hooke test lane 给出 fail-closed 矩阵；独立 Averroes review no High/Medium，唯一 Low（stdout `gate_status` 命名混淆）已修为 `workflow_gate_status`/`bundle_gate_status` 并复测。按分层验证策略未跑 `mix precommit`。
- **2026-06-14 续作：S3 MFA Delete 强制 + lifecycle 永久删保护**：新增 `Maxio.S3.MFADelete` 与 `Maxio.S3.MFADelete.Verifier` behaviour facade；`PutBucketVersioning` 解析后、落库前强制：含 `<MfaDelete>` 时必须同时含 `<Status>`、必须 root principal、必须 HTTPS 且已签名的 `x-amz-mfa`，并通过 `config :maxio, :mfa_delete_verifier`，未配置 verifier fail-closed；MFA Delete 已启用的桶改变 versioning 状态同样要求 root + MFA。`DELETE ?versionId`、`Suspended` 桶中会替换 `null` 版本的无 `versionId` DELETE、以及含 `<VersionId>` 或会替换 `null` 版本条目的 `DeleteObjects` 在 MFA Delete 桶中要求 root principal + MFA；`Enabled` 桶普通无 `versionId` DELETE 只插入独立 delete marker，不要求 MFA。`DeleteObjects` 缺/错 MFA 或非 root protected delete 为整请求失败，不落 per-key 部分成功。相邻授权修正：单删与批删指定 VersionId 现在要求 `s3:DeleteObjectVersion`，不再用 `s3:DeleteObject` 放行永久版本删除。为避免非 HTTP lifecycle 绕过，`Maxio.Lifecycle` 对 MFA Delete 桶跳过 `NoncurrentVersionExpiration`、`ExpiredObjectDeleteMarker` 以及 `Suspended` current Expiration 这类永久删除版本动作，summary/CLI/scheduler 日志新增 `mfa_delete_protected_actions_skipped` 计数。仍 defer：MFA 设备注册/轮换/TOTP/硬件 serial 管理、Console/operator 路径的交互式 MFA 语义。验证：official AWS docs 核对 PutBucketVersioning/DeleteObject/DeleteObjects/MFA Delete 语义；独立 Socrates review 指出 `Suspended/null` 与 unsigned `x-amz-mfa` 绕过风险后已补回归并修复；独立 Galileo review 指出 non-root protected delete 绕过后已补 root gate 与回归；独立 Jason review 指出 gate/执行版本状态双快照 race 后已改为同一 `bucket_meta.versioning` 快照；focused `mix test test/maxio/s3/mfa_delete_test.exs` → 18 tests, 0 failures；相邻 S3 versioning/authz 批次 `mix test test/maxio/s3/mfa_delete_test.exs test/maxio/s3/versioning_object_test.exs test/maxio/s3/delete_objects_versioning_test.exs test/maxio/s3/delete_objects_authz_test.exs test/maxio/s3/authz_matrix_test.exs` → 56 tests, 0 failures；lifecycle 批次 `mix test test/maxio/lifecycle_test.exs test/maxio/lifecycle/scheduler_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_lifecycle_run_test.exs` → 21 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors`、`mix format --check-formatted`、scoped `git diff --check` 通过。按分层验证策略未跑 `mix precommit`。
- **2026-06-14 续作：S3 MFA Delete 本地 TOTP 设备管理 / DB-backed verifier**：新增 `mfa_devices` 表（表/列/索引中文 COMMENT）与 `Maxio.IAM.MFADevice` schema，root/IAM user TOTP 设备以 `pending -> active -> disabled` 生命周期管理；TOTP seed 用 `Maxio.IAM.MFASecret` AES-256-GCM 信封加密，AAD 绑定 `x-amz-mfa`/STS `SerialNumber` serial，dev/test 固定 key，prod 缺 `MAXIO_MFA_SECRET_KEY` fail-fast。新增 `Maxio.IAM.TOTP`（RFC 6238 SHA1/30s/6 位，默认 ±1 step，constant-time compare）、`Maxio.IAM.MFADeleteVerifier`（默认 `config :maxio, :mfa_delete_verifier`，只接受 root principal，所有异常/缺设备/错码 fail-closed）、`last_accepted_step` 条件 `UPDATE` 原子消费 matched step 防同 code 并发重放。CLI 新增 `mix maxio.iam.mfa.create|activate|list|verify|disable|delete`：create 仅一次性输出 Base32 secret/otpauth URI；list/verify/disable/delete 不输出 seed；verify 默认 non-consuming，`--consume` 才烧码。独立 Fermat review 指出 prod 固定 key、AAD 前未 normalize serial、create 错误 inspect changeset 可能泄露 envelope，均已修复。仍 defer：硬件 MFA/硬件 serial 管理、AWS IAM MFA device API、轮换编排 UI、Console/operator 交互式 MFA。验证：`mix test test/maxio/iam/totp_test.exs test/maxio/iam/mfa_device_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_iam_mfa_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 13 tests, 0 failures；`mix test test/maxio/s3/mfa_delete_local_verifier_test.exs test/maxio/s3/mfa_delete_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 20 tests, 0 failures；相邻 S3 回归 `mix test test/maxio/s3/mfa_delete_local_verifier_test.exs test/maxio/s3/mfa_delete_test.exs test/maxio/s3/versioning_object_test.exs test/maxio/s3/delete_objects_versioning_test.exs test/maxio/s3/delete_objects_authz_test.exs test/maxio/s3/authz_matrix_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 58 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors`、scoped `mix format --check-formatted`、scoped `git diff --check` 通过。按分层验证策略未跑 `mix precommit`。
- **2026-06-14 续作：STS/session token 鉴权基础**：新增 `sts_sessions` 表（表/列/索引中文 COMMENT）、`Maxio.IAM.STSSession`、`Maxio.STS` 与 `Maxio.Auth.SessionToken`；本地 STS session credential 以独立表保存，不混入长期 `access_keys`，签发时只返回一次性 secret/session token，DB 仅保存 token SHA256。`CredentialStore.get_secret/2` 现在接收 `session_token` 与 `now`，EctoStore/MemoryStore 均 fail-closed 校验：临时 key 缺 token、错 token、过期、撤销拒绝，长期 key 携带 stale token 拒绝为 `InvalidToken`。SigV4 plug 提取 header `x-amz-security-token` 与 presigned `X-Amz-Security-Token`；header token 必须列入 `SignedHeaders`，presigned token 进入 canonical query；S3 错误映射补 `InvalidToken`/`ExpiredToken`。CLI 新增 `mix maxio.sts.issue_session|list_sessions|revoke_session`，list/revoke 不泄露 secret/token/hash。独立 Dalton review 指出 mixed-case `SignedHeaders` 可绕过 canonical token 覆盖、STS raw changeset 可能泄露 secret/hash、长期/临时 key 命名空间碰撞，均已修复并补回归。当轮 defer：HTTP Query API（后续续作已补 GET/POST form `GetSessionToken`）、AssumeRole/trust policy、WebIdentity、session policy/tags、Console UI、secret at-rest 加密统一治理。验证：`mix test test/maxio/auth/credential_store/memory_store_test.exs test/maxio/sts_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 10 tests, 0 failures；`mix test test/maxio/s3/session_token_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 6 tests, 0 failures；`mix test test/mix/tasks/maxio_sts_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 4 tests, 0 failures；review 修复后 `mix test test/maxio/sts_test.exs test/maxio/s3/session_token_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 13 tests, 0 failures；最终同层回归 `mix test test/maxio/auth_test.exs test/maxio/s3/plugs/sigv4_test.exs test/maxio/auth/credential_store/memory_store_test.exs test/maxio/sts_test.exs test/maxio/s3/session_token_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_sts_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 32 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors`、scoped `mix format --check-formatted`、scoped `git diff --check` 通过。按分层验证策略未跑 `mix precommit`。
- **2026-06-14 续作：STS GetSessionToken Query API（GET）**：在 S3 Endpoint 的 `SigV4` 之后、`Dispatch` 之前新增 `Maxio.STS.QueryPlug`，只拦截 service-level `Action` query，避免 `GET /?Action=...` 误落到 S3 `ListBuckets`。`GET /?Action=GetSessionToken&Version=2011-06-15&DurationSeconds=N` 现在返回 AWS STS Query XML `<GetSessionTokenResponse>`；请求必须已签名、必须使用 SigV4 `sts` service scope，且必须是长期凭据。临时凭据递归调用被拒绝且不会新增 `sts_sessions` 行；`DurationSeconds` 复用 `Maxio.STS.issue_session/1` 的 900 秒下限与 root/IAM principal 上限；未知 STS Action、错误 Version、未签名请求均返回 STS `<ErrorResponse>`，不会进入 S3 Authz/handler。测试 helper `Maxio.Test.SigV4Request` 支持 `service: "sts"`，端到端测试覆盖签发后临时凭据继续访问 S3、Action 不落 S3、duration 边界、临时凭据递归拒绝、STS scope 强制。独立 Linnaeus 只读设计/风险审查建议的管线位置、风险边界与最小测试集均已纳入；仍 defer：AssumeRole/trust policy、WebIdentity、session policy/tags、Console UI、secret at-rest 加密统一治理。验证：`mix test test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 6 tests, 0 failures；相邻回归 `mix test test/maxio/auth_test.exs test/maxio/s3/plugs/sigv4_test.exs test/maxio/auth/credential_store/memory_store_test.exs test/maxio/sts_test.exs test/maxio/s3/session_token_test.exs test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_sts_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 38 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors`、scoped `mix format --check-formatted` 通过。按分层验证策略未跑 `mix precommit`。
- **2026-06-14 续作：STS GetSessionToken Query API（POST form）**：`Maxio.STS.QueryPlug` 现支持 POST `application/x-www-form-urlencoded` Query API；service-level POST 在签名 scope 为 `sts` 或 content-type 为 form 时由 STS plug 接管，不会因缺/错 content-type、缺失 Action 或未知 Action 回落到 S3 `MethodNotAllowed`。POST form 只接受 header SigV4 + `:single` payload mode；`x-amz-content-sha256` 必须是实际 form body 的 64-hex SHA256，读取后用常数时间比较，拒绝 `UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD`、streaming payload、presigned POST form、重复 form 参数、超 64 KiB body 与非 form content-type。独立 Sagan 只读设计/风险审查指出的 payload hash 绑定、重复参数、content-type 与 fallback 风险均已纳入。仍 defer：WebIdentity、session policy/tags、Console UI、secret at-rest 加密统一治理。验证：`mix test test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 16 tests, 0 failures；相邻 STS/Auth/SigV4 回归 `mix test test/maxio/auth_test.exs test/maxio/s3/plugs/sigv4_test.exs test/maxio/auth/credential_store/memory_store_test.exs test/maxio/sts_test.exs test/maxio/s3/session_token_test.exs test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_sts_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 48 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors`、scoped `mix format --check-formatted` 通过。按分层验证策略未跑 `mix precommit`。
- **2026-06-14 续作：STS AssumeRole 基础闭环**：新增 `iam_roles` 表（表/列/索引中文 COMMENT）与 `Maxio.IAM.Role`，`IAM.create_role/get_role_by_arn/list_roles/delete_role` 管理 role + trust policy；`Policy.Statement.parse_trust_policy/2` 将 role ARN 注入 Resource 后复用统一 evaluator，且不经过 S3 Authz owner/root 短路。`Maxio.STS.assume_role/3` 签发 `assumed_role` 临时凭据，DB 仍只保存 token hash；CredentialStore 还原为 role ARN principal，使 S3 权限由绑定在 role ARN 上的 `principal_policies` 决定。`Maxio.STS.QueryPlug` 支持 GET/POST `Action=AssumeRole`，必需 `RoleArn`/`RoleSessionName`，可选 `DurationSeconds`；未知参数以及 `Policy`/`PolicyArns`/裸 `Tags`/`TransitiveTagKeys`/`ProvidedContexts` 均显式拒绝，避免静默扩大权限；service scope 为 `sts` 的缺 Action GET 不再落到 S3。仍 defer：WebIdentity、session policy、`TransitiveTagKeys` 链式 session tag 传播、ProvidedContexts、role Console UI 与 secret at-rest 加密统一治理；ExternalId/SourceIdentity/root/IAM user 本地 MFA trust context 与 role chaining 已由后续条目收敛，`Tags.member.N.Key/Value` 已由后续条目收敛。验证：`mix test test/maxio/iam_test.exs test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 32 tests, 0 failures；修复独立 Descartes review 后 `mix test test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 24 tests, 0 failures；相邻 IAM/STS/Auth/SigV4/Policy/S3 授权回归 `mix test test/maxio/iam_test.exs test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs test/maxio/sts_test.exs test/maxio/auth_test.exs test/maxio/s3/plugs/sigv4_test.exs test/maxio/auth/credential_store/memory_store_test.exs test/maxio/policy/store_test.exs test/maxio/policy/store_optional_callbacks_test.exs test/maxio/s3/identity_policy_authz_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 72 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors`、scoped `mix format --check-formatted`、`git diff --check` 通过。按分层验证策略未跑 `mix precommit`。
- **2026-06-14 续作：STS AssumeRole trust context（ExternalId / SourceIdentity / MFA）**：`Maxio.STS.QueryPlug` 现允许并验证 `ExternalId`、`SourceIdentity`、`SerialNumber`、`TokenCode`，分别映射到 trust policy 条件键 `sts:ExternalId`、`sts:SourceIdentity`、`aws:MultiFactorAuthPresent`；不完整 MFA 参数、非法 ExternalId/SourceIdentity 直接 STS `InvalidParameterValue`，MFA 验证失败返回 `AccessDenied`。MFA 使用现有 DB-backed root/IAM user TOTP 设备，先 non-consuming 验证参与 trust policy，再在签发前 consuming 验证，失败 fail-closed；`sts_sessions` 新增 `source_identity`、`mfa_authenticated`、`mfa_serial_hash`（列 COMMENT），只保存 serial SHA256，不保存 raw serial/token code。CredentialStore 会把 `source_identity` 还原到 session principal，S3 授权上下文新增 `aws:SourceIdentity`；MFA 布尔态只做审计和 trust-policy 取证，按 AWS 语义不对 `assumed_role` 后续 S3 请求注入 `aws:MultiFactorAuthPresent`。`AssumeRoleResponse` 回显 `<SourceIdentity>`。独立 Einstein 只读审查建议的参数边界、pre-issue consume 防重放、SourceIdentity S3 条件透传、raw serial 不落库均已纳入；独立 Lovelace 只读复审指出 AssumeRole MFA 传播到后续 S3 授权会偏离 AWS，已按 AWS 修正并补回归。仍 defer：硬件 MFA、ProvidedContexts、session policy、`TransitiveTagKeys`、WebIdentity；role chaining 已由后续条目收敛。验证：`mix test test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 30 tests, 0 failures；相邻 IAM/STS/Auth/SigV4/Policy/S3 授权回归 `mix test test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs test/maxio/sts_test.exs test/maxio/iam/mfa_device_test.exs test/maxio/s3/identity_policy_authz_test.exs test/maxio/auth_test.exs test/maxio/s3/plugs/sigv4_test.exs test/maxio/auth/credential_store/memory_store_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 64 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors`、scoped `mix format --check-formatted`、`git diff --check` 通过。按分层验证策略未跑 `mix precommit`。
- **2026-06-14 续作：STS GetCallerIdentity Query API**：`Maxio.STS.QueryPlug` 新增 `GET/POST Action=GetCallerIdentity`，继续复用 STS Query 管线的 Version 校验、SigV4 `sts` service scope 强制、POST exact payload hash/重复参数/64 KiB 上限；该 action 不调用 `reject_temporary_credentials`，因此长期凭据、`GetSessionToken` 临时凭据与 `AssumeRole` role session 均可调用。实现不评估 IAM identity policy、不签发 session、不写 DB、不消费 MFA，只把 `conn.assigns.principal` 渲染为 STS XML `UserId/Account/Arn`；AssumeRole session 返回 STS assumed-role ARN，不误回 IAM role ARN，`UserId` 优先用当前 role id + session name。`Account` 集中到 `config :maxio, :sts, account_id`，运行时可用 `MAXIO_ACCOUNT_ID` 覆盖，默认 `000000000000`；当前仍沿用 maxio 单账户内部 ARN 与 access-key surrogate，完整 AWS account-id / unique-id / role-id 语义随多租户 IAM 扩展收敛。独立 Feynman 只读审查建议的临时凭据放行、unknown action fallback、STS scope、assumed-role ARN 与 account-id 风险均已纳入或文档化。验证：`mix test test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 37 tests, 0 failures；相邻 IAM/STS/Auth/SigV4/Policy/S3 授权回归 `mix test test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs test/maxio/sts_test.exs test/maxio/iam/mfa_device_test.exs test/maxio/s3/identity_policy_authz_test.exs test/maxio/auth_test.exs test/maxio/s3/plugs/sigv4_test.exs test/maxio/auth/credential_store/memory_store_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 71 tests, 0 failures；scoped `mix format --check-formatted`、`mix compile --warnings-as-errors`、scoped `git diff --check` 通过。按分层验证策略未跑 `mix precommit`。
- **2026-06-14 续作：STS GetSessionToken MFA 参数 + IAM user 本地 TOTP 绑定**：`GetSessionToken` 现接受 `SerialNumber`/`TokenCode`，参数形状错误返回 `InvalidParameterValue 400`，MFA 设备缺失、principal mismatch、错码、replay 等验证失败返回 `AccessDenied 403`；实现先 non-consuming 验证确认上下文，再把 consuming 验证挂入 `STS.issue_session/1` 的 `pre_issue`，确保同一个 TOTP code replay 不会签发新 session。`mfa_devices` 允许绑定 root 与 IAM user，`verify_mfa_device/4` 继续按 exact principal ARN 匹配；S3 MFA Delete verifier 仍只接受 root principal。GetSessionToken session 会持久化 `mfa_authenticated` 与 serial SHA256，不保存 raw serial/token；后续 S3 授权对非 assumed-role session 注入 `aws:MultiFactorAuthPresent=true/false`，并保留 AssumeRole session 不传播该键的 AWS 语义。新增端到端回归覆盖 IAM user 无 MFA 被 Bool true 条件隐式拒绝、带 MFA 放行、同 code replay 拒绝、以及 broad Allow + explicit Deny `aws:MultiFactorAuthPresent=false` 的绕过防护。独立 Nash/Laplace 只读审查建议的两阶段 consume、IAM user 端到端证明、explicit Deny false 语义与迁移注释均已纳入。验证：`mix test test/maxio/iam/mfa_device_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 5 tests, 0 failures；`mix test test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 39 tests, 0 failures；相邻 IAM/STS/Auth/SigV4/MFA Delete/Policy/S3 授权回归 `mix test test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs test/maxio/sts_test.exs test/maxio/iam/mfa_device_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_iam_mfa_test.exs test/maxio/s3/mfa_delete_local_verifier_test.exs test/maxio/s3/identity_policy_authz_test.exs test/maxio/auth_test.exs test/maxio/s3/plugs/sigv4_test.exs test/maxio/auth/credential_store/memory_store_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 79 tests, 0 failures；scoped `mix format --check-formatted`、`mix compile --warnings-as-errors`、scoped `git diff --check` 通过。按分层验证策略未跑 `mix precommit`。
- **2026-06-14 续作：STS AssumeRole role chaining**：`AssumeRole` 现在允许 `GetSessionToken` 临时凭据与 assumed-role 临时凭据调用；`GetSessionToken` 仍拒绝临时凭据递归调用。链式 AssumeRole 的 `DurationSeconds` 按 AWS role chaining 语义收紧为最大 3600 秒，普通长期凭据 AssumeRole 继续按角色 `max_session_duration`。链式调用的 trust policy principal 以源 role ARN 参与匹配，新 session 的 S3 授权 principal 切换为目标 role ARN，不继承源 role policy；SourceIdentity 会跨链继承并同时暴露为 trust context 的 `sts:SourceIdentity` / `aws:SourceIdentity`，传入不同 SourceIdentity 直接 `InvalidParameterValue`，不签发 session。仍显式拒绝 `Policy`、`PolicyArns`、裸 `Tags`、`ProvidedContexts` 等未实现参数；SourceIdentity 调用方 `sts:SetSourceIdentity` identity-policy side 已由后续条目收敛，`Tags.member.N.Key/Value` 已由后续条目收敛，跨账户/account-principal 场景下的 `sts:AssumeRole` 调用方授权仍 defer。独立 Huygens 只读设计/语义审查建议的 3600 秒上限、目标 role 权限切换、SourceIdentity 继承与 `aws:SourceIdentity` trust key 均已纳入。验证：`mix test test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 40 tests, 0 failures；相邻 STS/Auth/SigV4/Policy/S3 授权回归 `mix test test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs test/maxio/sts_test.exs test/maxio/s3/identity_policy_authz_test.exs test/maxio/auth_test.exs test/maxio/s3/plugs/sigv4_test.exs test/maxio/auth/credential_store/memory_store_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 69 tests, 0 failures；scoped `mix format --check-formatted`、`mix compile --warnings-as-errors`、scoped `git diff --check` 通过。按分层验证策略未跑 `mix precommit`。
- **2026-06-14 续作：STS `sts:SetSourceIdentity` trust-policy gate**：role trust policy parser 现在允许 `sts:SetSourceIdentity` alongside `sts:AssumeRole`；当 AssumeRole 请求显式设置 SourceIdentity 或 role chaining 继承 SourceIdentity 时，`Maxio.STS.assume_role/3` 会在核心层统一注入 `sts:SourceIdentity` / `aws:SourceIdentity` 条件并额外评估 `sts:SetSourceIdentity`，不依赖 HTTP QueryPlug 调用者手写 conditions。目标 role trust policy 缺少 `sts:SetSourceIdentity`、或显式 Deny 该 action 时返回 `AccessDenied` 且不落 `sts_sessions`；未设置/未继承 SourceIdentity 的 AssumeRole 仍只要求 `sts:AssumeRole`。独立 Ptolemy 只读审查指出的直接调用绕过风险、role chaining 继承负例和文档边界均已修复。当轮仍 defer 的调用方 `sts:SetSourceIdentity` identity-policy side 已由后续条目收敛；跨账户/account-principal 场景下的 `sts:AssumeRole` 调用方授权仍 defer。验证：`mix test test/maxio/sts_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 7 tests, 0 failures；`mix test test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 42 tests, 0 failures；相邻 STS/Auth/SigV4/Policy/S3 授权回归 `mix test test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs test/maxio/sts_test.exs test/maxio/s3/identity_policy_authz_test.exs test/maxio/auth_test.exs test/maxio/s3/plugs/sigv4_test.exs test/maxio/auth/credential_store/memory_store_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 72 tests, 0 failures；scoped `mix format --check-formatted`、`mix compile --warnings-as-errors`、scoped `git diff --check` 通过。按分层验证策略未跑 `mix precommit`。
- **2026-06-14 续作：STS SourceIdentity 调用方 identity-policy gate**：`Maxio.STS.assume_role/3` 在目标 role trust policy 通过后，针对显式设置或链式继承 SourceIdentity 的非 root 调用方，额外用调用方绑定的 identity policy 评估 `sts:SetSourceIdentity`，资源为目标 role ARN；缺 Allow 或显式 Deny 时返回 `AccessDenied` 且不落 `sts_sessions`。该检查位于 STS 核心层而非仅 QueryPlug，因此 CLI/IEx/内部调用无法绕过；root 保持当前单账户兼容语义。role chaining 成功路径现在要求源 role policy 允许对目标 role `sts:SetSourceIdentity`，并保留目标 role trust policy 的同名 action gate。独立 Pasteur review 指出 direct/core role chaining 可通过 caller principal 自带 SourceIdentity 但 opts 不传绕过继承 gate；已把 SourceIdentity 继承/改写拒绝下沉到 `STS.assume_role/3` 核心层并补回归。仍 defer：跨账户/account-principal 场景下的 `sts:AssumeRole` 调用方 identity-policy side 授权、WebIdentity、session policy、`TransitiveTagKeys`、ProvidedContexts。验证：`mix test test/maxio/sts_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 9 tests, 0 failures；`mix test test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 42 tests, 0 failures；相邻 STS/Auth/SigV4/S3 授权回归 `mix test test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs test/maxio/sts_test.exs test/maxio/s3/identity_policy_authz_test.exs test/maxio/auth_test.exs test/maxio/s3/plugs/sigv4_test.exs test/maxio/auth/credential_store/memory_store_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 73 tests, 0 failures；scoped `mix format --check-formatted`、`mix compile --warnings-as-errors`、scoped `git diff --check` 通过。按分层验证策略未跑 `mix precommit`。
- **2026-06-14 续作：STS AssumeRole session tags + `sts:TagSession` trust gate**：`AssumeRole` Query API 现在支持 `Tags.member.N.Key/Value`，复用 `Maxio.Tags.validate(_, :bucket)` 的 50-tag/长度/字符约束，并额外拒绝大小写不敏感重复 tag key；保留前缀 `aws:` 现在大小写不敏感拒绝。目标 role trust policy parser 允许 `sts:TagSession`，且当请求携带 session tags 时必须同时允许 `sts:AssumeRole` 与 `sts:TagSession`；trust context 由 STS 核心层从结构化字段重建，包含 `aws:RequestTag/*`、`aws:TagKeys`、`aws:ResourceTag/*`、principal context/tag、ExternalId、SourceIdentity、MFA 与请求 envelope，不再信任调用方传入的整张 `opts[:conditions]`，避免 CLI/IEx/内部调用伪造 tag/MFA/ExternalId 条件。AssumeRole MFA 也下沉到核心层：QueryPlug 只传 `mfa_serial`/`mfa_token_code`，核心用 caller principal non-consuming 验证参与 trust policy，并在落库前 consuming；外部 `mfa_authenticated`/`mfa_time` 不再可伪造 trust context。session tags 会按大小写不敏感 key 覆盖 role tags 并持久化到 `sts_sessions.tags`，CredentialStore 还原后进入 S3 `aws:PrincipalTag/*` 授权上下文；Condition lookup 对 condition key 做大小写不敏感匹配，覆盖 `aws:PrincipalTag/Team` vs `team` 场景。独立 Peirce review 指出 direct/core 可通过 `opts[:conditions]` 伪造 `aws:RequestTag/*`、以及通过 `mfa_authenticated: true` 伪造 MFA trust context，均已把 trust context 重建/验证下沉到核心层并补 direct-call 回归。仍 defer：session policy、WebIdentity、ProvidedContexts。验证：`mix test test/maxio/sts_test.exs test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs test/maxio/policy/condition_test.exs test/maxio/tags_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 90 tests, 0 failures；相邻 STS/Auth/SigV4/Policy/S3 授权回归 `mix test test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs test/maxio/sts_test.exs test/maxio/policy/condition_test.exs test/maxio/tags_test.exs test/maxio/s3/identity_policy_authz_test.exs test/maxio/s3/authz_matrix_test.exs test/maxio/auth_test.exs test/maxio/s3/plugs/sigv4_test.exs test/maxio/auth/credential_store/memory_store_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 137 tests, 0 failures；scoped `mix format --check-formatted`、`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过。按分层验证策略未跑 `mix precommit`。
- **2026-06-14 续作：STS `TransitiveTagKeys` 链式 session tag 传播**：`AssumeRole` Query API 现在支持 `TransitiveTagKeys.member.N`，只允许把本次请求中显式传入的 session tag key 标记为 transitive；裸 `TransitiveTagKeys`、0 下标、重复/非法 key、无对应 `Tags.member.N.Key` 的 transitive key 均返回 STS `InvalidParameterValue`，不会签发 session。`sts_sessions` 新增 `transitive_tag_keys` 数组列（中文 COMMENT），`Maxio.IAM.STSSession` 与 `EctoStore` 会持久化/还原该状态；role chaining 时核心只继承 caller session 中已标记 transitive 的 tag，不会把纯 role tag 当作 transitive 传播。继承来的 transitive tag 会覆盖目标 role tag，但如果新请求显式传入同 key session tag，则 fail-closed 返回 `InvalidParameterValue` 且不落库；新 session 的 transitive keys 为继承 keys 与本次请求 keys 的大小写不敏感并集。trust context 新增 `sts:TransitiveTagKeys` 条件键，现按最终会继续传播的 key 集合（继承 + 本次请求）注入，使目标 role trust policy 可限制进入下一跳 session 的传播集合；`aws:RequestTag/*` 与 `aws:TagKeys` 仍只来自本次显式 session tags；只要有效 session tag 通过链路传播，目标 role trust policy 仍必须允许 `sts:TagSession`。独立 Plato 只读设计审查建议的独立数组字段、EctoStore principal 还原、QueryPlug 参数边界和 role tag 不传播风险均已纳入；独立 Euler 只读实现审查指出 `sts:TransitiveTagKeys` trust context 只含本次请求、未含继承 key 的 Medium，已修复并补 target trust policy `sts:TransitiveTagKeys=team` 与 role-only 不传播回归。验证：`mix test test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 45 tests, 0 failures；相邻核心/条件回归 `mix test test/maxio/sts_test.exs test/maxio/policy/condition_test.exs test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 81 tests, 0 failures；scoped `mix format --check-formatted`、`mix compile --warnings-as-errors`、scoped `git diff --check` 通过。按分层验证策略未跑 `mix precommit`。
- **2026-06-14 续作：STS AssumeRole inline session policy（Policy）交集授权**：`AssumeRole` Query API 现在接受 GET/POST `Policy` inline session policy，继续显式拒绝 `PolicyArns`；STS 核心用 identity-policy parser 校验 JSON、禁止 Principal/空 Statement/缺 Action 或 Resource/超过 2048 字节，失败返回 `MalformedPolicyDocument` 且不落 `sts_sessions`。`sts_sessions` 新增 `session_policy` JSONB/map 列（中文 COMMENT），`Maxio.IAM.STSSession` 与 `EctoStore` 会持久化/还原并在凭据加载时重新 parse，异常 fail-closed。S3 授权层把 session policy 作为 permissions boundary，而不是 union 进 `Store.principal_policies/1`：对携带非空 `session_policy_statements` 的 assumed-role principal，必须同时满足 role identity policy allow 与 inline session policy allow，且两者都用 `owner?: false` 评估，避免 bucket owner/root 短路把 session policy 变成扩权；主授权、PutObjectTagging、ACL header 二次授权、CopyObject source read 与 DeleteObjects per-key/owner branch 均走同一 helper。新增 E2E 覆盖 role allow+session shrink、session 不能扩权、role explicit Deny on owned bucket、ACL header/CopyObject/DeleteObjects owner branch 不能被 session policy 单独放行、畸形 Policy 不签发凭据、PolicyArns 仍拒绝。独立 Socrates 复审首轮指出 owner/root 短路仍可绕过 role identity policy，已修复为 role identity ∩ session policy 双重检查并补回归；复审 no must-fix。验证：`mix test test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 53 tests, 0 failures；`mix test test/maxio/sts_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 11 tests, 0 failures；相邻授权回归 `mix test test/maxio/s3/identity_policy_authz_test.exs test/maxio/s3/copy_object_authz_test.exs test/maxio/s3/delete_objects_authz_test.exs test/maxio/s3/acl_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 35 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors`、scoped `mix format --check-formatted`、scoped `git diff --check` 通过。按分层验证策略未跑 `mix precommit`。
- **2026-06-14 续作：STS AssumeRole caller-side `sts:AssumeRole` / `sts:TagSession` identity-policy gate（本地 account-principal trust 收敛）**：STS 核心现在区分 direct principal trust 与本地 account/root-principal trust：direct principal trust 仍按 AWS 同账户语义不要求 caller identity Allow，但 caller identity policy 的 `sts:AssumeRole` explicit Deny 必须胜出；本地 account/root-principal trust fallback 则要求非 root caller identity policy 对目标 role ARN 显式 Allow `sts:AssumeRole`，explicit Deny/implicit Deny 均返回 `AccessDenied` 且不落 `sts_sessions`。Trust policy explicit Deny 会同时以真实 caller context 与本地 account/root context 预检查，确保 root/account principal Deny 压过 direct allow；携带 session tags 时，非 root caller 还必须在 identity policy 中允许 `sts:TagSession`，并复用同一 STS trust/request 条件上下文。QueryPlug 对 caller assume/tag denial 映射为 STS XML `AccessDenied` 403，不落 internal failure。验证：`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；`mix test test/maxio/sts_test.exs test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 71 tests, 0 failures；相邻 Auth/SigV4/identity-policy 批次 `mix test test/maxio/auth_test.exs test/maxio/s3/plugs/sigv4_test.exs test/maxio/auth/credential_store/memory_store_test.exs test/maxio/s3/identity_policy_authz_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 23 tests, 0 failures；scoped `mix format --check-formatted`、scoped `git diff --check` 通过。独立 Dewey review 首轮指出 direct trust 绕过 caller explicit Deny、account/root Deny 未压过 direct allow、session tag 缺 caller `sts:TagSession` identity gate，均已修复并补回归；复审 no blocker。按用户效率要求未跑 `mix precommit`。
- **2026-06-14 续作：STS AssumeRole `PolicyArns` managed session policies（签发时快照为 boundary）**：新增 `managed_policies` 表（表/列/索引/timestamps 中文 COMMENT）与 `Maxio.Policy.ManagedPolicy`，`Maxio.Policy.Store` 增加 optional `put_managed_session_policy/2`、`managed_session_policy/1`，EctoStore 用独立表存取 identity-policy 文档并校验禁止 Principal/空 Statement/畸形 JSON；旧 policy store 后端缺 callback 时返回 `:not_supported`，不破坏 behaviour。`AssumeRole` Query API 现在接受 `PolicyArns.member.N.arn`，继续拒绝裸 `PolicyArns`/坏 member 字段；本轮只支持本地 canonical `arn:aws:iam:::policy/<path>`，拒绝跨账户/AWS managed/non-IAM ARN。STS 核心按 AWS session policy 语义限制 inline `Policy` + policy ARN 明文总量 ≤2048 bytes、managed policy 最多 10 个、重复/缺失/畸形引用 fail-closed；签发时把 inline 与 managed policy statements 合并快照到既有 `sts_sessions.session_policy`，后续 S3 授权仍走 role identity policy ∩ session policy boundary，managed policy 后续修改不会扩大已签发 session。新增回归覆盖 managed policy 缩权、不能扩权、快照语义、缺失/非法/超过 10 个 PolicyArns 不签发、Store optional callback。验证：`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；目标 `mix test test/maxio/sts_test.exs test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs test/maxio/policy/store_test.exs test/maxio/policy/store_optional_callbacks_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 87 tests, 0 failures；相邻授权 `mix test test/maxio/s3/identity_policy_authz_test.exs test/maxio/s3/copy_object_authz_test.exs test/maxio/s3/delete_objects_authz_test.exs test/maxio/s3/acl_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 35 tests, 0 failures；最终受影响 Query API `mix test test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 61 tests, 0 failures；scoped `mix format --check-formatted`、scoped `git diff --check` 通过。独立 Leibniz design lane 指出不应把 managed policy 伪装进 `principal_policies` registry，已改为独立 `managed_policies` 表并补迁移/schema；窄复审 agent Huygens 超时未返回结论，未跑 `mix precommit`。
- **2026-06-14 续作：IAM managed policy 管理面 + CLI（供 `PolicyArns` 引用）**：在上一条 `PolicyArns` 内部 registry 基础上补齐正式管理面：`Maxio.IAM.managed_policy_arn/2`、`validate_managed_policy_arn/1`、`create_managed_policy/1`、`get/list/delete_managed_policy` 与保留给 fixture/seeding 的 upsert helper；`create` 通过新增 `Maxio.Policy.Store.create_managed_session_policy/2` 走 Ecto 原子 insert，DB 唯一约束把重复映射为 `{:error, :already_exists}`，避免 check-then-upsert 并发覆盖 future STS boundary。`managed_policies.policy_arn` 改为 `text` 并补 forward migration，新增按 ARN 最后一段 policy name 的大小写不敏感唯一索引（中文 COMMENT），对齐 IAM policy name 同账号内 case-insensitive unique；ARN 校验按 path 512 字节 + policy name 128 字节分别执行，STS `PolicyArns` 入口复用同一 validator，新增边界回归覆盖 path 512 + name 128 能持久化并被 AssumeRole 使用。新增 CLI：`mix maxio.iam.managed_policy.create --name NAME [--path PATH] (--file PATH | --policy JSON) [--json]`、`get --arn ARN`、`list [--path-prefix PATH] [--with-document]`、`delete --arn ARN`；list 默认不展开 document。验证：`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；`mix test test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 62 tests, 0 failures；本切片聚焦集合 `mix test test/maxio/iam_test.exs test/maxio/policy/store_test.exs test/maxio/policy/store_optional_callbacks_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_iam_managed_policy_test.exs test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 90 tests, 0 failures；scoped `mix format --check-formatted`、scoped `git diff --check` 通过。独立 Helmholtz review 首轮指出原子 create、`put` name/path、case-insensitive uniqueness、ARN 边界 4 类问题；均已修复，最终复审 no blocker。按用户效率要求未跑 `mix precommit`。
- **2026-06-15 续作：IAM role attached managed policies（AttachRolePolicy / ListAttachedRolePolicies / DetachRolePolicy）**：新增 `iam_role_managed_policy_attachments` 表（role ARN + managed policy ARN，FK/唯一索引/反查索引/timestamps 中文 COMMENT）与 `Maxio.IAM.RoleManagedPolicyAttachment`；`Maxio.Policy.Store` 增加 role managed policy attach/list/detach/attached? optional callbacks。EctoStore 的 `principal_policies(%{arn: role_arn})` 现在只通过 attachment join 合并该 role 已绑定的 managed policy statements，未绑定的 `managed_policies` registry 不会泄露授权；这使 AssumeRole 还原出的 role ARN principal 在 S3 授权中自然获得 attached managed policies，同时仍保持 `PolicyArns` session boundary 快照语义。`IAM.attach_role_policy/2` 显式校验 role 与 policy 存在，attach 幂等；`detach_role_policy/2` 幂等；`delete_managed_policy/1` 和底层 `Store.delete_managed_session_policy/1` 对已绑定 policy 返回结构化 `{:error, :policy_attached}`，不静默 detach、不暴露 raw FK crash。新增 CLI：`mix maxio.iam.attach_role_policy --role-arn ARN --policy-arn ARN [--json]`、`mix maxio.iam.list_attached_role_policies --role-arn ARN [--path-prefix PATH] [--json] [--with-document]`、`mix maxio.iam.detach_role_policy --role-arn ARN --policy-arn ARN [--json]`。验证：`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；本切片聚焦集合 `mix test test/maxio/iam_test.exs test/maxio/policy/store_test.exs test/maxio/policy/store_optional_callbacks_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_iam_role_policy_test.exs test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 90 tests, 0 failures；scoped `mix format --check-formatted`、scoped `git diff --check` 通过。独立 Halley 设计审查确认 role attachment 应接入 `principal_policies(role_arn)` 且必须通过 attachment join，指出 delete attached policy 应结构化失败；已修复并补 Store/IAM 测试。独立 Confucius 窄复审 no blocker。按用户效率要求未跑 `mix precommit`。
- **2026-06-15 续作：UploadPartCopy 核心兼容切片**：`PUT ?partNumber&uploadId` 在携带 `x-amz-copy-source` 时不再落入普通 UploadPart body 路径，而是走服务端流式 copy part；新增 `Maxio.S3.CopySource` 统一解析 copy-source/versionId/source 条件头/range，新增 `CopyPartResult` view。UploadPartCopy 支持全量源对象、`x-amz-copy-source-range`（源对象需大于 5 MiB，按 AWS 语义 fail-closed）、源 `versionId`、delete marker 拒绝、SSE-S3 源解密后流式裁剪，并回 `x-amz-copy-source-version-id`。Authz 的 copy-source 二次授权扩展到 `:upload_part`：目标仍要求 `s3:PutObject`，源按 `versionId` 要求 `s3:GetObject` / `s3:GetObjectVersion`；普通 UploadPart 不受影响。`CopySource` 解析先切原始 `?` 再 decode，避免 `%3F` key 被误切为 query；COPY 条件语义补齐 `x-amz-copy-source-if-modified-since` 失败时 412。新增 multipart 集成回归覆盖全量 copy、`%3F` key、source If-Modified-Since、range copy、指定源版本 copy 与源读权限拒绝；CopyObject 也补 `%3F` key 回归。验证：`mix test test/maxio/s3/multipart_integration_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 15 tests, 0 failures；相邻 CopyObject 授权/版本回归 `mix test test/maxio/s3/copy_object_authz_test.exs test/maxio/s3/copy_object_version_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 14 tests, 0 failures；目标组合 `mix test test/maxio/s3/multipart_integration_test.exs test/maxio/s3/copy_object_authz_test.exs test/maxio/s3/copy_object_version_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 29 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors`、`mix format --check-formatted`、`git diff --check` 通过。独立 Boyle review 指出 source If-Modified-Since 被忽略、`%3F` key 解析误切两个问题，已修复并补回归。按用户效率要求未跑 `mix precommit`。
- **2026-06-15 续作：ListObjects V1 兼容切片**：`GET /bucket` 默认切到 S3 ListObjects V1；只有 `list-type=2` 走既有 ListObjectsV2，`list-type=1`/其它值/缺省均走 V1。Dispatcher 从仅看 query key 升级为值级 query map 判定，同时保留 MapSet 测试入口兼容；新增 `:list_objects` handler/action，复用 `Maxio.ObjectMeta.list/3` 元数据门面，把 V1 `marker` 映射为现有严格大于游标。新增 `Maxio.S3.Views.ListBucketV1`，输出 V1 `Marker`/`NextMarker`/`Contents`/`CommonPrefixes`，不输出 V2-only `KeyCount`、`ContinuationToken`、`NextContinuationToken`、`StartAfter`；`NextMarker` 仅在 delimiter + truncated 时返回，缺省截断让客户端按最后一个 Key 续页。Authz/ActionMap 已把 `:list_objects` 映射为 `s3:ListBucket`，并复用 `s3:prefix`/`s3:delimiter`/`s3:max-keys` 与 bucket resource tag 条件，避免默认入口绕过 bucket policy。新增 V1 view/e2e 回归覆盖默认 V1 XML、marker 分页、delimiter 下 NextMarker 与下一页不重复 common prefix、`list-type=1` 不误进 V2；Dispatcher/ActionMap 测试同步更新。独立 McClintock review 指出 delimiter `NextMarker` 可能重复分页、MapSet 单测误把 `list-type` 当 V2 值两个问题，已修复：V1 common-prefix marker 会推进到底层字典序上界，dispatcher 单测改用 map 值级断言。验证：AWS ListObjects 官方文档核验 marker/NextMarker/encoding-type 语义；`mix test test/maxio/s3/dispatcher_test.exs test/maxio/policy/action_map_test.exs test/maxio/s3/list_bucket_v1_view_test.exs test/maxio/s3/list_objects_v1_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 26 tests, 0 failures；相邻授权/encoding/view 回归 `mix test test/maxio/s3/authz_matrix_test.exs:88 test/maxio/s3/authz_matrix_test.exs:108 test/maxio/s3/authz_matrix_test.exs:203 test/maxio/s3/encoding_type_e2e_test.exs test/maxio/s3/views_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 13 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors`、scoped `mix format --check-formatted`、`git diff --check` 通过。按分层验证策略未跑 `mix precommit`。
- **2026-06-15 续作：PutObject checksum header 兼容切片**：新增 `Maxio.S3.Checksum`，PutObject 现在支持普通请求头 `Content-MD5`、`x-amz-checksum-md5`、`x-amz-checksum-crc32`、`x-amz-checksum-sha1`、`x-amz-checksum-sha256` 的流式校验；checksum 在 `BodyReader` 输出的 decoded 明文字节、SSE-S3 加密前计算，`Storage.put/5` 成功后、metadata commit 前验证，失败返回 `BadDigest` 并走既有孤儿字节回滚，不会提交对象元数据。`Content-MD5` 只校验不回显；`x-amz-checksum-*` 成功时按请求 checksum header 回显。`x-amz-sdk-checksum-algorithm` 现在必须带匹配的 checksum value header，否则 `InvalidRequest` / `BadDigest` fail-closed；未知/未实现 checksum header（CRC32C/CRC64NVME/SHA512 等）与 checksum trailer 继续 `NotImplemented`。UploadPart/UploadPartCopy 在支持安全 part 回滚/原子 staged part 前统一 pre-write 拒绝 checksum header/trailer，避免坏 part 覆盖旧字节后保留旧 metadata。独立 Ptolemy/Kuhn 只读审查指出的 no-op finalizer 绕过、`Content-MD5` nil 响应头、`x-amz-sdk-checksum-algorithm` 静默忽略、UploadPartCopy 未拒绝、失败路径 checksum Agent 泄漏均已收敛；trailer checksum 与 UploadPart checksum 正式支持仍 defer。验证：`mix test test/maxio/s3/checksum_e2e_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 9 tests, 0 failures；相关上传/分段回归 `mix test test/maxio/s3/integration_test.exs test/maxio/s3/multipart_integration_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 51 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors`、scoped `mix format --check-formatted`、`git diff --check` 通过。按分层验证策略未跑 `mix precommit`。
- **2026-06-15 续作：UploadPart checksum header 兼容切片**：UploadPart body 路径现在复用 `Maxio.S3.Checksum` 支持普通请求头 `Content-MD5`、`x-amz-checksum-md5/crc32/sha1/sha256` 与 `x-amz-sdk-checksum-algorithm` 匹配校验；checksum 包装在 `BodyReader` decoded 字节流外层，存储后端写入 temp/staged part 并计算 `%{size, etag, md5_raw}` 后，通过新的 `opts[:before_part_commit]` hook 在 final part commit 前验证，成功时回显请求的 `x-amz-checksum-*` 响应头，失败返回 `BadDigest` 且不更新 multipart part metadata。FS/ErasureCoded/Distributed 的 `put_part/6` 均改为 staged write：hook 失败或 body/checksum 异常会清理 temp，不覆盖既有 final part；`Storage.Backend` 明确记录该 precommit hook 契约。ErasureCoded/Distributed 的 part replacement commit 增加同盘/同端点 backup→commit→cleanup/restore 事务语义；Distributed 额外把不可达 `get_sidecar` 与缺失 `:enoent` 区分开，旧 part 状态不可确认时在 precommit 阶段返回 `{:error, :write_quorum}`，quorum 成功但部分 endpoint commit 失败时清理失败 endpoint final，避免恢复上线后暴露旧 part 或 torn final。UploadPartCopy checksum header/trailer 继续 fail-closed 为 `501 NotImplemented`，避免 copy 路径静默忽略 checksum；普通 UploadPartCopy 不受影响。新增 shared storage conformance 覆盖 FS/EC/Distributed 在 hook 失败后同一 partNumber 旧字节仍可读，新增 atomic commit 回归覆盖 EC/Distributed 低于 quorum 恢复旧 part、Distributed 旧 endpoint 不可确认时 abort、quorum 成功但部分 endpoint 失败不保留旧 ETag，新增 S3 E2E 覆盖 UploadPart checksum 成功回显、BadDigest 不覆盖旧 part 并可用旧 ETag 完成 MPU、UploadPartCopy checksum 失败后 ListParts 为空。验证：`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；focused 批次 `mix test test/maxio/cluster/transport_in_process_test.exs test/maxio/storage/fs_conformance_test.exs test/maxio/storage/erasure_coded_conformance_test.exs test/maxio/storage/distributed_conformance_test.exs test/maxio/storage/atomic_commit_test.exs test/maxio/s3/checksum_e2e_test.exs test/maxio/s3/multipart_integration_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 70 tests, 0 failures；scoped `mix format --check-formatted`、`git diff --check` 通过。独立 Raman review 指出 EC/Distributed replacement commit quorum 失败会破坏旧 part、Distributed 不可达 endpoint 会被误判为缺失等阻塞，已修复并补回归；独立 Galileo 最终复审无 blocker/high，结论“可以提交”。按分层验证策略未跑 `mix precommit`。
- **2026-06-15 续作：aws-chunked checksum trailer 兼容切片**：`Maxio.Auth.SigV4.StreamingVerifier` 现在支持 `STREAMING-UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD-TRAILER` 与 `STREAMING-AWS4-HMAC-SHA256-PAYLOAD-TRAILER`，严格核对 trailer 声明与实际 header，trailer canonicalization 走小写 `name:value\n`，终止 trailer 后任何额外字节均 fail-closed。`BodyReader.stream_with_trailers/2` 暴露 verified trailer getter/discarder，`Maxio.S3.Checksum` 可用 trailer 满足 `x-amz-sdk-checksum-algorithm`，并对 PutObject/UploadPart 的 MD5/SHA1/SHA256/CRC32 trailer 做 decoded 明文字节校验；未知算法继续 `501 NotImplemented`，UploadPartCopy checksum trailer 仍 fail-closed。PutObject checksum/content-sha256/decoded-length 校验改为通过新的 `opts[:before_object_commit]` 在 FS/ErasureCoded/Distributed final object commit 前执行，避免坏 trailer 在对象已提交后才失败；ErasureCoded 的 PutObject precommit 异常路径已补 `cleanup_tmps/1`，并新增 atomic 回归确保旧对象保留且 `.tmp` 清空。验证：`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；目标集 `mix test test/maxio/auth/sigv4/streaming_verifier_test.exs test/maxio/s3/body_reader_test.exs test/maxio/s3/checksum_test.exs test/maxio/s3/checksum_e2e_test.exs test/maxio/storage/fs_conformance_test.exs test/maxio/storage/erasure_coded_conformance_test.exs test/maxio/storage/distributed_conformance_test.exs test/maxio/storage/atomic_commit_test.exs test/maxio/s3/integration_test.exs test/maxio/s3/multipart_integration_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 116 tests, 0 failures；scoped `mix format --check-formatted`、`git diff --check` 通过。独立 Locke review 首轮指出 PutObject 校验晚于 final commit、Agent 泄漏、extra trailer bytes、ErasureCoded temp 泄漏等 blocker，均已修复；最终复核无 blocking findings。按用户要求采用分层验证，未跑 `mix precommit`。
- **2026-06-15 续作：GetObjectAttributes 基础兼容切片**：`GET /bucket/key?attributes` 从 dispatcher `not_implemented` 升级为真实对象级 action，handler 支持 `x-amz-object-attributes` 中已有权威数据可真实返回的 `ETag`、`ObjectSize`、`StorageClass`；缺失 header 返回 `InvalidRequest`，非法 attribute 返回 `InvalidArgument`。当前对象表/MPU 元数据尚未持久化完整对象 checksum 或 completed part attributes，因此 `Checksum` / `ObjectParts` 明确 fail-closed 为 `501 NotImplemented`，且在条件头 304/412 判定前拒绝，避免用条件请求绕过未实现属性。响应 XML 根节点按 AWS 文档使用 `GetObjectAttributesResponse`，200 响应回 `Last-Modified`，`StorageClass` 对 `STANDARD` 对象省略。版本语义复用既有 `VersionId.normalize_param`、`validate_version_id!` 与 delete-marker 处理：latest delete marker 返回 404 + `x-amz-delete-marker:true`，显式 delete marker version 返回 405 + `Last-Modified`/version headers。Authz 映射新增 `s3:GetObjectAttributes`，带 `versionId` 时切换为 `s3:GetObjectVersionAttributes`，并按 AWS 要求做第二道 read 权限检查：非版本请求还需 `s3:GetObject`，版本请求还需 `s3:GetObjectVersion`；`s3:ExistingObjectTag` / `aws:ResourceTag` object tag 条件同读类对象动作接入。新增 e2e 覆盖 metadata 返回、缺失 header、unsupported 属性、条件头 fail-closed、STANDARD storage class 省略、非 owner 双权限、versionId 双权限与 delete-marker 语义。验证：AWS GetObjectAttributes 官方文档核验权限、response shape、Last-Modified、StorageClass 规则；目标集 `mix test test/maxio/s3/object_attributes_test.exs test/maxio/s3/dispatcher_test.exs test/maxio/policy/action_map_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 31 tests, 0 failures；相邻授权/对象回归 `mix test test/maxio/s3/identity_policy_authz_test.exs test/maxio/s3/authz_matrix_test.exs test/maxio/s3/integration_test.exs test/maxio/s3/versioning_object_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 75 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors`、scoped `mix format --check-formatted`、`git diff --check` 通过。独立 Nietzsche review 首轮指出 unsupported 属性可被条件头绕成 304/412、XML root/Last-Modified 不兼容、STANDARD StorageClass 应省略，均已修复并补回归；按分层验证策略未跑 `mix precommit`。
- **2026-06-16 续作：Bucket OwnershipControls 兼容切片**：`GET/PUT/DELETE /bucket?ownershipControls` 从 dispatcher `not_implemented` 升级为真实 bucket config action，新增 `Maxio.S3.OwnershipControls`、XML parser/view/handler 与 `bucket_configs.ownership_controls` 持久化列（迁移带中文 COMMENT）。CreateBucket 现在按现代 S3 默认写入 `BucketOwnerEnforced`；显式 `x-amz-object-ownership: ObjectWriter|BucketOwnerPreferred` 写入对应配置，其中 `ObjectWriter` 用于保留 ACL 可用语义；DeleteBucketOwnershipControls/历史无配置桶仍回退到 maxio 既有 ACL 可用行为，避免破坏兼容。`BucketOwnerEnforced` 下 ACL 不再参与授权，GetBucketAcl/GetObjectAcl 返回 owner-only 视图；PutBucketAcl、PutObjectAcl 返回 `AccessControlListNotSupported` 400；PutObject/CopyObject/CreateMultipartUpload 携带 ACL header 时除 `bucket-owner-full-control` 外均拒绝且不落对象/会话；CreateBucket 默认 BOE + public ACL/grant 返回 `InvalidBucketAclWithObjectOwnership`，已有非 owner-only bucket ACL 时也不能切换到 BOE。ActionMap/Authz 映射新增 `s3:PutBucketOwnershipControls`、`s3:GetBucketOwnershipControls`，DELETE 按 AWS 要求复用 `s3:PutBucketOwnershipControls`，CreateBucket 带 ownership header 追加要求 `s3:PutBucketOwnershipControls`、带 ACL header 追加要求 `s3:PutBucketAcl`，并纳入 bucket resource tag 条件键。验证：聚焦 `mix test test/maxio/s3/request_parsers/ownership_controls_test.exs test/maxio/s3/ownership_controls_test.exs test/maxio/s3/dispatcher_test.exs test/maxio/bucket_configs_test.exs test/maxio/policy/action_map_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 37 tests, 0 failures；相邻 ACL/PAB/Authz/MPU/STS 回归 `mix test test/maxio/s3/acl_test.exs test/maxio/s3/public_access_block_test.exs test/maxio/s3/authz_test.exs test/maxio/s3/authz_matrix_test.exs test/maxio/s3/multipart_integration_test.exs test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 138 tests, 0 failures；格式化后关键回归 `mix test test/maxio/s3/request_parsers/ownership_controls_test.exs test/maxio/s3/ownership_controls_test.exs test/maxio/s3/acl_test.exs test/maxio/s3/public_access_block_test.exs test/maxio/s3/authz_test.exs test/maxio/s3/authz_matrix_test.exs test/maxio/s3/multipart_integration_test.exs test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` → 149 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile
